Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The approximation operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 317. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 237 with 3 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_317(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 317. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^237(S, F) is trivial. QED.
